### [101] [Equivalent potential-energy functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00378)
*Francisco M. Fernández*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一套简单规则来确定双原子分子势能函数之间的等价性，并通过多个例子进行了系统说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多研究者提出了各自的双原子分子势能函数，其中一些已被证明是等价的，但缺乏统一的判定方法。

Method: 应用少量简单规则，建立确定分子势能函数之间等价关系的系统化程序。

Result: 通过多个示例展示了该系统性程序的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的简单规则可以系统化地用于判定双原子分子势能函数的等价性。

Abstract: Many researchers have proposed their own potential-energy functions for the study of diatomic molecules, some of which have been proved to be equivalent. In this paper we suggest the application of a few simple rules to determine the equivalence between molecular potentials. The resulting systematic procedure is illustrated by means of several examples.

</details>


### [102] [Weyl-type theorems in Galilei and Carroll geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00799)
*Philip K. Schwartz,James Read,Quentin Vigneron*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Weyl定理在伽利略几何与卡罗尔几何（即洛伦兹几何的非相对论和超相对论极限）中的类似结果，表明在适当定义的共形结构下，无挠线性连接可由其射影结构唯一确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Weyl定理（即Weyl度量由其共形结构和射影结构唯一确定）推广到伽利略几何与卡罗尔几何中，这两种几何分别对应非相对论极限和超相对论极限下的时空几何。

Method: 通过定义适合伽利略和卡罗尔几何的共形结构概念，研究在这些几何框架下无挠线性连接与其射影结构之间的唯一确定关系。

Result: 在伽利略几何与卡罗尔几何中，适当定义的共形结构下，存在与Weyl定理类似的结论，即无挠线性连接可由其射影结构唯一确定。

Conclusion: Weyl定理可以推广到非相对论和超相对论极限下的时空几何中，为理解这些几何中连接与结构之间的关系提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A classic theorem of Weyl (1921) states that a Weyl metric -- a natural generalisation of a pseudo-Riemannian metric -- is uniquely determined by its conformal and projective structures (i.e. by its conformal structure and its set of unparametrised geodesics). An equivalent formulation of Weyl's result is that a torsion-free linear connection compatible with a pseudo-Riemannian conformal structure is uniquely determined by its projective structure. We discuss analogous results for suitably defined notions of conformal structure for Galilei and Carroll geometry, i.e. for spacetime geometries arising as the `non-relativistic' and `ultra-relativistic' limits of Lorentzian geometry.

</details>


### [103] [The 2D Smorodinsky--Winternitz II system and the Laguerre--Heun algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00903)
*Vutha Vichea Chea,Luc Vinet,Alexei Zhedanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别了二维Smorodinsky-Winternitz II系统的二次对称代数与Laguerre型合流Heun代数之间的等价关系，给出了该超可积系统的一个直接实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Smorodinsky-Winternitz II系统背后的代数结构，建立其与Heun代数之间的联系。

Method: 通过分析系统在Cartesian坐标和抛物坐标下的可分离性，识别出互补Cartesian分离算子Y为Laguerre型，抛物积分W为L_2（代数Heun伙伴），并导出生成元Y、W、Z=[Y,W]满足的对易关系。

Result: 得到了一个超可积的Laguerre-Heun代数实现，其中H为中心元。

Conclusion: 该系统的二次对称代数等价于Laguerre型合流Heun代数，为相关可积系统提供了代数框架。

Abstract: We identify the quadratic symmetry algebra of the two-dimensional Smorodinsky--Winternitz II system with a Laguerre-type confluent Heun algebra. The system is separable in Cartesian and parabolic coordinates. The complementary Cartesian separation operator \[
  Y=\partial_y^2-ω^2y^2+\frac{1/4-c^2}{y^2} \] is of Laguerre type, while the parabolic integral \(W=L_2\) is its algebraic Heun partner. With \(Z=[Y,W]\), the defining relations are \[
  [Y,Z]=16ω^2W-2bY,\qquad
  [W,Z]=6Y^2-4HY+2bW+8ω^2(1-c^2), \] where \(H\) is central. This gives a direct superintegrable realization of the Laguerre--Heun algebra.

</details>


### [104] [A first-order formulation for axisymmetric Willmore surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01001)
*Z. C. Tu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了轴对称Willmore曲面的首次积分形式，将其降阶为一阶常微分方程，并基于该方程提供了曲面分类方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将轴对称Willmore曲面的控制方程从二阶降为一阶，便于分类和分析该类曲面的几何性质。

Method: 通过结合两个独立的首次积分，推导出关于距离ρ和角度函数Ψ的一阶常微分方程，其中包含两个积分常数C₁和C₂。

Result: 得到了一个包含两个积分常数的首次积分方程，将原二阶方程降为一阶，并以球面和Clifford环面作为基本验证案例。

Conclusion: 该一阶表述为Willmore旋转曲面的分类提供了便捷框架。

Abstract: We show that axisymmetric Willmore surfaces admit a first-order formulation obtained by combining two independent first integrals. If $ρ$ denotes the distance from the axis of revolution and $Ψ=\sinψ$, where $ψ$ is the tangent angle of the generating curve, then the profile satisfies \begin{equation*} \frac{\left[Ψ(ρΨ'-Ψ)^2+2(ρΨ'-Ψ)+2C_1ρ\right]^2}{1-Ψ^2} +\left[(ρΨ'-Ψ)^2-2\right]^2=C_2, \end{equation*} where $C_1$ and $C_2$ are constants of integration and the prime denotes differentiation with respect to $ρ$. This equation reduces the axisymmetric Willmore equation to a first-order ordinary differential equation and provides a convenient classification scheme for Willmore surfaces of revolution. The sphere and the Clifford torus are discussed as elementary checks of the formulation.

</details>


### [105] [On Jean-Marie Souriau's geometric quantization of the relativistic electron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01121)
*Géry de Saxcé*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Souriau《动力系统结构》中关于几何量子化的章节，证明其中两个关键定理以赋予预量子流形辛和接触结构，并将其应用于自旋1/2相对论粒子，导出狄拉克方程、概率流守恒，提出自旋流守恒恒等式及基于Kaluza-Klein理论的CPT对称性系统构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Souriau的《动力系统结构》中几何量子化章节的许多重要结果和公式缺乏证明，难以推导，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 回顾余伴随轨道方法及其在相对论性自旋粒子中的应用；陈述并证明两个关键定理，使预量子流形具备辛结构和接触结构；将上述理论应用于自旋1/2相对论粒子；利用Kaluza-Klein理论系统构造电荷共轭、宇称变换和时间反演对称性。

Result: 推导出狄拉克方程和概率流守恒；提出自旋流守恒恒等式；给出了比经典表述更便捷清晰的CPT对称性构造方案。

Conclusion: 本文严格证明了Souriau几何量子化框架中的关键定理，为相对论性自旋粒子的量子化提供了严谨的数学基础，并改进了CPT对称性的构造方法。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to revisit, in Souriau's book "Structure of Dynamical Systems", the chapter devoted to the geometric quantization where the justifications of important results and formulae are not given and are difficult to prove. After recalling the coadjoint orbit method and its application to the relativistic particle with spin, we state and prove two keystone theorems that allow to equip its prequantum manifold with the symplectic and contact structures. We apply them to the relativistic particle with spin 1/2, leading to the Dirac equation and the conservation of the probability current. We propose also an identity of conservation of the spin current and, invoking the Kaluza-Klein theory, a systematic construction of the symmetries of charge conjugation, parity transformation and time reversal which seems to us more convenient and readable than that of the classical presentations.

</details>


### [106] [The Schwinger-Dyson equations for random fuzzy geometries coupled to matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01343)
*Jeremy Gamble,Masoud Khalkhali,Nathan Pagliaroli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与费米子或玻色子耦合的(0,1)型随机模糊几何的Schwinger-Dyson方程与鞍点方程，推导了双迹厄米矩阵系综的解析解，并在高斯模型中用椭圆积分严格表达了自由能和一阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和求解与费米子或玻色子耦合的随机模糊几何中出现的矩阵积分方程，这类系统来自双迹厄米矩阵系综且包含行列式贡献。

Method: 利用复分析技术从鞍点方程推导Schwinger-Dyson方程，对任意势能项进行迭代求解；对高斯模型用椭圆积分严格表达自由能和一阶矩。

Result: 得到了任意势能下Schwinger-Dyson方程的迭代求解方案；对高斯模型（单玻色子或单费米子）给出了自由能和一阶矩的椭圆积分形式的严格公式。

Conclusion: 玻色子情形与Hoppe模型及三色模型密切相关，为随机模糊几何的矩阵模型解析求解提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: In this work we study the Schwinger-Dyson equations and saddle point equations of matrix integrals that come from type $(0,1)$ random fuzzy geometries coupled to fermions or bosons. Such random fuzzy geometries are bi-tracial Hermitian matrix ensembles with a determinant contribution in the integrand. We derive the Schwinger-Dyson equations using complex analytic techniques from the saddle point equation. For arbitrary potentials with either bosonic or fermionic contributions, their Schwinger-Dyson equations can be solved iteratively. For both the Gaussian models with either one boson or fermion we rigorously derive the formula for the free energy and first moment in terms of elliptic integrals. In the bosonic case this solution is closely related to the Hoppe model and the three-colour model.

</details>


### [107] [A review on the kinetic theory of oscillator chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01358)
*Pierre Germain,Joonhyun La,Angeliki Menegaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了一维非线性振子链（以Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou方程为最著名例子）的动理学理论，详细介绍了从微观到介观、介观到宏观的极限推导过程（动理学波动方程与流体力学极限），并讨论了与FPUT悖论和傅里叶定律推导两大经典问题的关联，最后提出了若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理一维非线性振子链的动理学理论，特别是从微观到宏观的多尺度极限推导，并揭示其与FPUT悖论及傅里叶定律推导等数学物理核心问题的联系。

Method: 采用非严格但详细的推导方式，阐述微观→介观（动理学波动方程）和介观→宏观（流体力学极限）两个极限过程，并纳入现有数学理论（含严格证明）的综述。

Result: 呈现了动理学波动方程的推导及流体力学极限的完整图像，总结了相关数学理论的进展状态，并明确了FPUT悖论与傅里叶定律推导两个问题在该框架下的位置。

Conclusion: 一维非线性振子链的动理学理论在连接微观动力学与宏观输运方面取得了重要进展，但仍存在大量开放问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We review the kinetic theory of one-dimensional nonlinear oscillator chains, of which the most famous example is the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou equation. We provide detailed, though not rigorous, accounts of the microscopic to mesoscopic, and mesoscopic to macroscopic limits: derivation of the kinetic wave equation and hydrodynamic limit. We also present the state of the art of the mathematical theory, including proofs. We discuss the connection to two famous problems of Mathematical Physics: the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou paradox, and the derivation of Fourier's law. Finally, many open problems and possible directions for future research are proposed.

</details>


### [108] [Exact spectrum of the XX spin chain with constrained non-diagonal boundary fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01918)
*Changqing Liu,Xiaotian Xu,Xin Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有约束非对角边界场的XX自旋链的精确谱，通过Bethe Ansatz方程分析。研究发现Bethe根具有确定奇偶性且位于单值函数的零点，基态与第一激发态的Bethe根构型基于数值观察得到，元激发由一对Bethe根协同变化表征，并获得了热力学极限下基态能量的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解具有约束非对角边界场的XX自旋链的精确谱，理解边界场对系统可积性和能谱结构的影响。

Method: 采用Bethe Ansatz方法建立方程，通过分析Bethe根的奇偶性约束和零点位置，结合数值观察研究基态和激发态的根构型。

Result: 发现Bethe根具有确定奇偶性；元激发由一对Bethe根协同变化表征；获得了热力学极限下基态能量的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 本文成功解析了约束非对角边界场下XX自旋链的精确谱，揭示了元激发的成对协同变化机制，为理解非对角边界可积系统提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the exact spectrum of the XX spin chain with constrained non-diagonal boundary fields, which can be analyzed by solving the associated Bethe Ansatz equations. In these equations, the number of Bethe roots has a definite parity, and all Bethe roots are located at the zeros of a unary function. We investigate the possible positions of the Bethe roots. Based on numerical observations, we analyze the Bethe root configurations for the ground state and the first excited state. Our results show that elementary excitations are characterized by the cooperative change of a pair of Bethe roots. Furthermore, we obtain an analytical expression for the ground state energy in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [109] [Painlevé XXXIV Asymptotics for the Focusing mKdV Equation with Finite-Genus Background and Discrete Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01977)
*Ruihong Ma,Engui Fan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了聚焦型修正KdV方程在有限亏格代数-几何拟周期初值下的Cauchy问题，通过Deift-Zhou非线性最速下降法，导出了在临界区域（复稳态相点与有限亏格分支割线端点碰撞）的长时渐近表达式，该展开一致有效至O(t^{-1/2})误差阶，领头项包含有限亏格代数-几何背景及由背景慢调制的呼吸子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚焦型mKdV方程在有限亏格拟周期初值下的长时渐近行为，特别是处理复稳态相点与分支割线端点碰撞的临界情形，该问题在先前工作中尚未被解决。

Method: 采用Deift-Zhou非线性最速下降法分析关联的Riemann-Hilbert问题；通过局部Painlevé XXXIV参数化处理临界点碰撞；将离散谱（呼吸子）纳入渐近分析框架。

Result: 得到了临界区域中一致有效至O(t^{-1/2})误差阶的长时渐近展开式，领头项为有限亏格代数-几何背景叠加呼吸子，且呼吸子参数受背景解慢调制。

Conclusion: 成功建立了聚焦型mKdV方程在有限亏格拟周期初值下临界区域的长时渐近理论，揭示了呼吸子与代数-几何背景的耦合调制机制。

Abstract: We investigate the Cauchy problem for the focusing modified Korteweg--de Vries (mKdV) equation with finite-genus algebro-geometric quasi-periodic initial data. By applying the nonlinear steepest-descent method of Deift--Zhou to the associated Riemann--Hilbert (RH) problem, we derive the long-time asymptotics of the solution in the critical regime where complex stationary phase points coalesce with the endpoints of the finite-genus branch cuts. The collision is resolved via a local Painlevé XXXIV parametrix, and the discrete spectrum (breathers) is incorporated into the analysis. The resulting expansion is valid uniformly up to an error of order $\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})$. In this critical region, the leading-order term comprises the finite-genus algebro-geometric background together with breathers, whose parameters are slowly modulated by the background solution.

</details>


### [110] [Higher-Rank Orthogonal Twists, APS Boundary Conditions, and $O(2)$-Equivariant Spectral Flow on a Warped Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02186)
*Taro Kimura,Sanchita Sharma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限扭曲圆柱上Dirac算子的O(2)-等变谱流，通过将正交扭转束复化并对角化，将Dirac方程分解为标量Fourier模径向方程，得到RO(O(2))值谱流的显式分块公式，并讨论了秩三情形及端点η/APS指标解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的谱流理论主要处理整数值谱流，缺乏对O(2)等变对称性下表示论信息的精细刻画。本文旨在建立O(2)-等变谱流公式，以捕捉被整数值谱流丢失的表示论信息。

Method: 将正交扭转束复化并对角化，将Dirac方程分解为标量Fourier模径向方程，包含移动旋转块和静止中性块；重排共轭对和反射配对块后，交叉贡献定义实RO(O(2))-类；在标准自伴Fredholm、端点可逆性和正则交叉假设下，通过组装分离块的局部交叉贡献得到显式分块公式。

Result: 得到了RO(O(2))值谱流的显式分块公式，该公式细化了一般整数值谱流，并展示了维度映射RO(O(2))→Z如何丢失表示论信息。讨论了秩三情形中固定中性扇区的作用及端点η/APS指标解释。

Conclusion: 本文建立的O(2)-等变谱流公式能够更精细地刻画对称性对谱流的影响，为理解等变指标理论提供了新的工具，尤其在处理带有反射对称性的几何系统中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We study $O(2)$-equivariant spectral flow for Dirac operators on a finite warped cylinder equipped with fixed admissible regularized APS boundary conditions. The twisting bundle is a real higher-rank orthogonal bundle, and reflection symmetry is implemented by a fiber involution. After complexifying the twisting bundle and diagonalizing the orthogonal twist, the Dirac equation decomposes into a scalar Fourier-mode radial equation, with moving rotating blocks and stationary neutral blocks. After regrouping conjugate and reflection-paired blocks, the crossing contributions define real $RO(O(2))$-classes. Consequently, we obtain an explicit blockwise formula for the $RO(O(2))$-valued spectral flow of the resulting regularized APS family. Under the standard self-adjoint Fredholm, endpoint-invertibility, and regular-crossing hypotheses, together with a fixed neutral-sector convention, this formula is obtained by assembling the local crossing contributions of the separated blocks. It refines ordinary integer-valued spectral flow and shows explicitly how the dimension map $RO(O(2))\to\mathbb Z$ loses representation-theoretic information. We also discuss the rank-three case, including the role of the fixed neutral sector, and the corresponding endpoint $η$/APS index interpretation.

</details>


### [111] [Exponential thermalisation of viscous fluids on negatively curved manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02286)
*Samuel L. Braunstein,Zhi-Wei Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在具有严格负Ricci曲率的紧致黎曼流形上提出了随机Navier-Stokes方程，利用形变拉普拉斯算子并通过拓扑论证（Poincaré引理）推导涨落-耗散关系。证明谱截断系统唯一稳态分布为Gibbs测度，收敛至平衡的指数速率由形变拉普拉斯谱隙下界决定（≥κ²当Ric≤-κ²g），且该速率不依赖于区域体积。负曲率空间上速度-速度关联函数呈指数衰减，与平坦空间的代数衰减形成对比，为负曲率流形上的粘性流体提供了严格的统计力学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性不可压Navier-Stokes方程在物理上不完备：有限温度下的粘性流体必然存在热涨落，其形式由涨落-耗散关系决定。现有理论未能将这类热涨落纳入严格的数学框架，尤其是在弯曲空间几何背景下。

Method: (1) 在紧致负Ricci曲率黎曼流形上引入形变Laplacian构造随机Navier-Stokes方程；(2) 利用Poincaré引理从拓扑角度推导涨落-耗散关系，唯一确定噪声项；(3) 对谱截断系统分析稳态分布与收敛速率；(4) 研究速度-速度关联函数的几何依赖性。

Result: 谱截断系统的唯一稳态分布为Gibbs测度（模态振幅高斯分布）；收敛至平衡的指数速率至少为2νλ_D，其中λ_D ≥ κ²（当Ric ≤ -κ²g时），且与区域体积无关；负曲率空间上速度-速度关联函数呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 为负曲率流形上的粘性流体提供了严格的统计力学基础，揭示了区域几何不仅控制确定性动力学，还控制热平衡的趋近过程。负曲率空间的热化速率在无限体积极限下保持非零，而平坦空间则趋于零，凸显了几何在统计力学中的关键作用。

Abstract: The deterministic incompressible Navier-Stokes equations are physically incomplete: any viscous fluid at finite temperature must exhibit thermal fluctuations whose form is dictated by the fluctuation-dissipation relation. We formulate the stochastic Navier-Stokes equations with the kinematically selected deformation Laplacian on compact Riemannian manifolds with strictly negative Ricci curvature. The fluctuation-dissipation relation, derived from a topological (Poincaré lemma) argument, uniquely determines the noise from the viscous operator. For the spectrally truncated system, we prove that the unique stationary distribution is the Gibbs measure (Gaussian in the mode amplitudes, because the nonlinear convective terms preserve energy), and that convergence to equilibrium is exponentially fast with rate at least $2νλ_\Def$, where $ν$ is the kinematic viscosity and $λ_\Def$ is the spectral gap of the deformation Laplacian. The spectral gap satisfies $λ_\Def \geq κ^2$ when $\Ric \leq -κ^2 g$, and is independent of the volume of the domain. On flat space, the analogous thermalisation rate vanishes in the infinite-volume limit. The equilibrium velocity-velocity correlation function decays exponentially in geodesic distance, in contrast to the algebraic decay on flat space. These results provide a rigorous statistical-mechanical foundation for viscous fluids on negatively curved manifolds and illustrate how the geometry of the domain controls not only the deterministic dynamics but also the approach to thermal equilibrium.

</details>


### [112] [There Are No Dirac Mass Terms with de Sitter Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02477)
*Craig McRae*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在de Sitter群旋量表示中，满足必要性质的算符必然导致费米子自耦合为奇宇称，从而排除了任何de Sitter对称的自旋-1/2费米子理论中的标准狄拉克质量项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lorentz正交群的表示论中，宇称算符P和時間反演算符T分别被实现为线性算符和反线性算符是经典结论。本文旨在考察de Sitter群旋量表示中这些算符的性质及其对费米子质量项的影响。

Method: 通过分析de Sitter群旋量表示中满足宇称和时间反演必要性质的算符，推导出这些算符对费米子自耦合的约束。

Result: 唯一满足de Sitter群旋量表示所需性质的算符导致费米子自耦合必然是奇宇称的，标准狄拉克质量项（偶宇称）被排除。

Conclusion: 任何de Sitter对称的自旋-1/2费米子理论中都无法容纳标准的狄拉克质量项。

Abstract: In the representation theory of Lorentzian orthogonal groups there are well known arguments as to why the parity operator $\mathcal{P}$ and the time reversal operator $\mathcal{T}$ should be realized as linear and anti-linear operators respectively (Wigner 1932). In this paper it is shown that the only operators satisfying the requisite properties for the spinor representation of the de Sitter group lead to fermion self-couplings which are necessarily parity odd, ruling out standard Dirac mass terms from any de Sitter symmetric theory of spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ fermions.

</details>

### [113] [Structure, Composition, and High-Field Superconductivity in Metal-Rich $\mathrmη$-Carbide-Type Compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00894)
*Manuele Balestra,KeYuan Ma,Harald O. Jeschke,Fabian O. von Rohr*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了η-碳化物型超导相的研究进展，这类材料表现出异常高的上临界场，显著违背弱耦合Pauli极限，其超导性质受晶体对称性、过渡金属组成和电子结构等因素调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η-碳化物型化合物作为新兴的金属-rich量子材料家族，展现出从复杂金属键中涌现的非常规超导特性，有必要系统梳理其研究进展并指出未来方向。

Method: 综述了Nb₄Rh₂C₁₋δ、Ta₄Rh₂C₁₋δ、Ti₄Ir₂O₁₋δ和Ti₄Co₂O₁₋δ等η-碳化物型超导体的实验发现，分析了晶体对称性、合成挑战、过渡金属组成及电子结构对超导性能的影响。

Result: η-碳化物型超导体的超导转变温度可达约10 K，上临界场高达约30 T，显著违背弱耦合Pauli极限，展现出晶体学高对称性特征。

Conclusion: η-碳化物型相是一类具有强抗Pauli极限破坏能力的超导材料体系，未来有待发现更多新成员并深入理解其非常规超导机制。

Abstract: $\mathrmη$-Carbide-type compounds have recently emerged as a diverse class of materials in the study of superconductivity. These phases contribute to a growing family of metal-rich quantum materials that exhibit unusual superconducting properties emerging from complex metallic bonding. Several members of the $\mathrmη$-carbide-type phases have been found to be bulk superconductors -- such as Nb$_4$Rh$_2$C$_{1-δ}$, Ta$_4$Rh$_2$C$_{1-δ}$, Ti$_4$Ir$_2$O$_{1-δ}$, and Ti$_4$Co$_2$O$_{1-δ}$ -- with transition temperatures up to $T_{\rm c} \approx$ 10 K and upper critical fields as high as $μ_0 H_{\rm c2}(0) \approx$ 30 T. Whereas the transition temperatures may fall within the range typical for intermetallic superconductors, the pronounced violation of the weak-coupling Pauli limit in many of these crystallographically high-symmetry materials is noteworthy. Here, we review recent progress on superconducting $\mathrmη$-carbide-type phases, emphasizing how crystal symmetry, synthetic challenges, transition-metal composition, and electronic structure govern their superconducting properties. Furthermore, we outline open questions and future directions, including the possible discovery of new $\mathrmη$-carbide-type materials.

</details>


### [114] [Interplay between Quantum Metric and Hybridized Collective Modes in Flat-Band Superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01235)
*Yi Liu,Mingyan Wang,Penghui Hu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了平带超流体中的集体激发，发现长波极限下仅存在单一低能集体模，该模具有二次色散（ω∝q²），其色散系数由正常态的量子度量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索平带超流体中配对涨落与密度涨落耦合驱动的集体激发行为，揭示与常规超流体不同的低能激发模式及其物理起源。

Method: 通过耦合配对（相位和振幅）与密度涨落的动力学，结合解析推导与数值计算，分析长波极限下的集体模色散关系。

Result: 时间反演对称的孤立平带超流体在长波极限下仅存在一个无隙但具有二次色散的低能集体模；色散系数由正常态的量子度量决定；解析与数值结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该结果普适适用于任意s波平带超流体（前提是平带与其他色散带能量分离良好），揭示了量子度量在平带超流体集体激发中的核心作用。

Abstract: We investigate collective excitations in flat-band superfluids by incorporating the coupled dynamics of pairing (phase and amplitude) and density fluctuations. We demonstrate that for any time-reversal symmetric superfluid system with an isolated flat band, only a single low-energy collective mode emerges in the long-wavelength limit. In contrast to the linearly dispersive Goldstone mode in conventional superfluids, this hybridized mode is gapless at zero momentum but exhibits a quadratic dispersion ($ω\propto q^2$) at small momenta. Analytically, we reveal that the dispersion coefficient of this collective mode is governed by the normal-state quantum metric of the flat band. These analytical predictions are in excellent agreement with numerical calculations. Our results are universally applicable to any generic $s$-wave flat-band superfluid, provided the flat band is energetically well separated from other dispersive bands.

</details>


### [115] [Excitonic-Superconducting Coexistence and Emergent Nematic Superconductivity Driven by Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01785)
*Fei Yang,Ruigang Li,Junwei Liu,Binghai Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过自洽微观理论证明，电子-空穴Fermi面失配会稳定FFLO-like电子-空穴配对并导致激子绝缘态自发对称性破缺，从而重构Fermi面不同区域的配对相空间，使激子绝缘序和超导序互补共存，并自发产生向列相超导态，无需外加对称性破缺场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为激子绝缘（EI）序和超导（SC）序是互斥的电子不稳定性，本文旨在从微观理论上澄清二者在存在Fermi面失配情况下的竞争与共存机制。

Method: 采用自洽微观理论框架，在存在超导配对的情况下系统研究激子绝缘相，分析电子与空穴Fermi面本征失配对二者竞争关系的重塑作用。

Result: Fermi面失配稳定了FFLO-like电子-空穴配对并驱动EI态自发对称性破缺；对称性破缺重构了SC和EI态的配对相空间，使Fermi面不同区域互补地支持EI或SC关联，实现两序共存；所导致的SC态自发打破旋转对称性，形成本征向列相超导，无需磁场、自旋-轨道耦合或能带各向异性等外场。

Conclusion: 单层1T'-MoTe₂和方网半金属NaAlSi是观测该现象的理想候选材料，该发现揭示了竞争多体序产生电子向列性的独特机制，为关联量子材料中自发各向异性电子态提供了新路径。

Abstract: Excitonic insulating (EI) and superconducting (SC) orders are generally regarded as mutually exclusive electronic instabilities. Within a self-consistent microscopic theory, we study electronic systems hosting an EI phase in the presence of SC pairing and show that an intrinsic mismatch between electron and hole Fermi surfaces fundamentally reshapes this competition. This mismatch stabilizes FFLO-like electron-hole pairing and drives spontaneous symmetry breaking of the EI state. The resulting symmetry breaking reconstructs the pairing phase space for SC and EI state, such that different regions of the Fermi surface complementarily support either EI or SC correlations, leading to a natural coexistence of the two orders. Notably, the emergent SC state consequently breaks rotational symmetry and develops intrinsic nematic superconductivity, even in the absence of explicit symmetry-breaking fields (such as magnetic fields, spin-orbit coupling, or bare band-structure anisotropy). Our results suggest that candidate materials such as monolayer 1T$'$-MoTe$_2$ and the square-net semimetal NaAlSi may provide promising platforms for observing this phenomenon. More broadly, these findings reveal a unique mechanism by which competing many-body orders generate electronic nematicity, suggesting a broader route toward spontaneous anisotropic electronic states in correlated quantum materials.

</details>


### [116] [Evolution of the intertwining correlated topological phases in iron-based superconductor Fe(Te,Se)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01786)
*Yue Sun,Shiying He,Zhongyi Zhang,Yong Huang,Jingheng Chen,Weixiang Yan,Chunbo Yu,Yuyang Dong,Kohei Aido,Xin Zhou,Zhengtai Liu,Mao Ye,Jishan Liu,Haruhisa Kitano,Zhixiang Shi,Hong Ding,Takeshi Kondo,Xianxin Wu,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用高分辨角分辨光电子能谱，在铁基超导体Co掺杂Fe(Te,Se)中直接解析出两种不同的交织拓扑态（拓扑绝缘体表面态和拓扑狄拉克半金属态），并研究了它们随电子掺杂的演化，揭示了强关联效应的影响，为探索多种拓扑超导态及其马约拉纳模的相互作用提供了理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种拓扑电子相可以在同一量子材料中共存，诱导不同的拓扑超导态，揭示拓扑超导态与马约拉纳模的相互作用，以及关联效应对其的影响。铁基超导体兼具拓扑态和关联效应，是研究这些现象的理想平台。

Method: 采用高分辨角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)技术，直接观测Co掺杂Fe(Te,Se)中的拓扑电子态，研究其随电子掺杂的演化规律。

Result: 识别出拓扑绝缘体表面态和拓扑狄拉克半金属态同时穿越费米能级的区域；发现拓扑态受强关联效应影响，与平庸体态分离；明确了不同拓扑相之间的演化关系。

Conclusion: 该体系为探索多种拓扑超导态之间的相互作用以及相关马约拉纳模提供了理想平台，有助于深入理解拓扑超导态与马约拉纳模的物理机制。

Abstract: Multiple topological electronic phases can coexist within a single quantum material and induce different topological superconducting states, offering deeper insights into interplay of topological superconducting states and Majorana modes, which may also be influenced and modified by correlation effect. Iron-based superconductors, with both topological states and correlation effect, is an ideal platform to study these phenomena. Here, with high resolution angle resolved photoelectron spectroscopy, we directly resolve two distinct intertwining topological states in iron-based superconductor Co-doped Fe(Te,Se), and study their evolution with electron doping. We identify a region where both topological insulator surface states and topological Dirac semimetal states intersect the Fermi level. The topological states are affected by the strong correlation effect and are isolated from trivial bulk states. The evolution between distinct topological phases offers a good opportunity to study various Majorana modes from different superconducting phases according to theoretical analysis. Our findings establish an ideal platform for exploring the interaction between multiple topological superconducting states and the related Majorana modes.

</details>


### [117] [Phonon-driven nodal surface superconductivity of Fermi arcs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02371)
*Francesco Buccheri,Alessandro de Martino,Jeroen van den Brink*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文提出时间反演不变的Weyl半金属中，费米弧电子与表面/体声子相互作用可导致表面超导态的形成，而体内仍保持非超导。研究发现库仑屏蔽效率决定两种配对通道（弧内/弧间）的竞争，费米弧的不连通性和弱屏蔽效应导致超导能隙中出现节点，这与PtBi₂的光电子能谱实验一致。通过表面涂层调控库仑屏蔽可工程化超导临界温度和能隙结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Weyl半金属中拓扑表面态可形成超导能隙而体态仍无超导这一反常现象的微观机制。

Method: 理论分析费米弧电子与表面/体声子相互作用，考虑两种配对通道（弧内配对与弧间配对）的竞争关系及库仑屏蔽效应的影响。

Result: 发现费米弧的不连通性和表面弱库仑屏蔽导致超导能隙中出现节点，该理论预测与PtBi₂的光电子能谱实验结果一致。

Conclusion: 表面库仑屏蔽是调控Weyl半金属表面超导的关键参数，可通过表面涂层等方式实现对临界温度和超导能隙结构的人工调控。

Abstract: According to recent observations, the topological surface states of Weyl semimetals may develop a superconducting gap, while bulk superconductivity remains absent. What drives the formation of this novel superconducting state is an open question. Here, we show that this phenomenon can arise from the interaction of Fermi arc electrons with both surface and bulk phonons in time-reversal-invariant Weyl semimetals. We identify two competing pairing channels, intra-arc and inter-arc, whose relative strength is governed by the efficiency of Coulomb screening at the surface. The combined effect of the Fermi arcs being disconnected and the weak screening of the Coulomb repulsion at the system's surface causes nodes to appear in the superconducting gap, as observed recently by photoelectron spectroscopy experiments on PtBi2. This suggests manipulation of the Coulomb screening, e.g. by a surface layer coating, as a pathway to engineer the critical temperature, as well as size and symmetry of the surface superconducting gap.
